src/core/dimensions.js
```javascript
/**
 * src/core/dimensions.js
 * Physical dimensions, KaTeX cancellation formatters, and universal number formatting.
 */

export const DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({
  LENGTH: "Length",
  FORCE: "Force",
  TORQUE: "Torque",
  POWER: "Power",
  PRESSURE: "Pressure / Stress",
  STRESS: "Pressure / Stress",
  VELOCITY: "Linear Velocity",
  ROT_SPEED: "Rotational Speed",
  PITCH: "Tooth Pitch",
  ANGLE: "Angle",
  COUNT: "Count",
  ENERGY: "Energy / Work",
  SPECIFIC_ENERGY: "Specific Energy / Enthalpy",
  MASS: "Mass",
  MASS_FLOW: "Mass Flow Rate",
  VOLUME: "Volume",
  VOLUME_FLOW: "Volume Flow Rate",
  SPECIFIC_CONSUMPTION: "Specific Fuel Consumption",
  TEMPERATURE: "Temperature",
  TEMPERATURE_DIFF: "Temperature Difference",
  PERCENTAGE: "Percentage",
  DIMENSIONLESS: "Dimensionless"
});

/**
 * Universal Number Formatter:
 * Enforces up to 6 significant figures, with a strict maximum of 3 decimal places.
 * Formats with thousands separators. Trims trailing zeroes to the right of the decimal.
 */
export function fmtSigFig(n, sigFigs = 6) {
  if (n === undefined || n === null) return "";
  const num = typeof n === "number" ? n : Number(n);
  if (isNaN(num)) return "";

  // Helper to add thousand separators to the integer part
  const addSeparators = (valStr) => {
    const parts = valStr.split(".");
    parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");
    return parts.join(".");
  };

  if (Number.isInteger(num)) return addSeparators(num.toString());

  // 1. Apply significant figures precision first
  let formatted = parseFloat(num.toPrecision(sigFigs));

  // 2. Enforce maximum of 3 decimal places across the entire repository
  if (Math.abs(formatted) < 1e-4) return "0";

  const str = formatted.toString();
  if (str.includes(".")) {
    const parts = str.split(".");
    if (parts[1].length > 3) {
      formatted = Number(formatted.toFixed(3));
    }
  }

  return addSeparators(formatted.toString());
}

export function fmt(n) {
  return fmtSigFig(n, 6);
}

/**
 * Given-value formatter. Measured givens round to 6 s.f.; constants bypass
 * rounding entirely and print their tabulated academic form (meta.display),
 * or full precision when no tabulated form is declared.
 * @param {number} value
 * @param {object} meta varsDb / CONSTANTS_DB entry for the variable
 */
export function formatGivenValue(value, meta = {}) {
  if (meta.isConstant) {
    if (typeof meta.display === "number") return String(meta.display);
    if (typeof meta.display === "string") return meta.display;
    return String(value);
  }
  return fmtSigFig(value, 6);
}

export function cancelUnit(unitStr) {
  if (!unitStr) return "";
  return `\\cancel{\\text{${unitStr}}}`;
}

export function textUnit(unitStr) {
  if (!unitStr) return "";
  if (unitStr === "%") return "%";
  if (unitStr.startsWith("°")) return unitStr;
  return `\\text{${unitStr}}`;
}

export function targetLabel(name, token, unit) {
  if (!unit || unit === "dimless" || unit === "ratio") {
    return `${name} (${token})`;
  }
  if (unit === "deg" || unit === "°") {
    return `${name} (${token}) in °`;
  }
  if (unit === "%") {
    return `${name} (${token}) in %`;
  }
  if (unit.startsWith("°")) {
    return `${name} (${token}) in ${unit}`;
  }
  return `${name} (${token}) in ${unit}`;
}
```

/**
 * src/cas/units/definitions.js
 * Comprehensive engineering unit registry with:
 * - Exact affine temperature offset transformations for °C and °F
 * - Support for vector temperature differences (bypassing affine origin offsets)
 * - Scaled percentage dimension (1% = 0.01)
 * - Single source for unit TeX (unitToTex) and value formatting
 */

export const BASE_DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({
  L: "Length", T: "Time", F: "Force", M: "Mass", Th: "Temperature", C: "Count", E: "Energy"
});

export const ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS = Object.freeze({
  m:     { dim: "L", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{m}" },
  mm:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.001, tex: "\\text{mm}" },
  cm:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.01, tex: "\\text{cm}" },
  in:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.0254, tex: "\\text{in}" },
  ft:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.3048, tex: "\\text{ft}" },
  yd:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.9144, tex: "\\text{yd}" },
  s:     { dim: "T", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{s}" },
  min:   { dim: "T", scale: 60.0, tex: "\\text{min}" },
  h:     { dim: "T", scale: 3600.0, tex: "\\text{h}" },
  hr:    { dim: "T", scale: 3600.0, tex: "\\text{h}" },
  N:     { dim: "F", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{N}" },
  kN:    { dim: "F", scale: 1000.0, tex: "\\text{kN}" },
  lb:    { dim: "F", scale: 4.4482216, tex: "\\text{lb}" },
  kip:   { dim: "F", scale: 4448.2216, tex: "\\text{kip}" },
  kg:    { dim: "M", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{kg}" },
  g:     { dim: "M", scale: 0.001, tex: "\\text{g}" },
  lb_m:  { dim: "M", scale: 0.45359237, tex: "\\text{lb}_m" },
  K:     { dim: "Th", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{K}" },

  "°F":  { dim: "Th", scale: 5 / 9, offset: 459.67, tex: "°\\text{F}" },
  "°R":  { dim: "Th", scale: 5 / 9, tex: "°\\text{R}" },
  Btu:   { dim: "E", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{Btu}" },
  teeth: { dim: "C", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{teeth}" },

  "kJ/kg-K":  { factor: 1000, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, kg: -1, K: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{K}}" },

  "Btu/lb-F":  { atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1, "°F": -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}\\cdot°\\text{F}}" }
});

export function getUnitAtomBag(uName) {
  if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") {
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: true };
  }
  if (uName === "%") {
    return {
      factor: 0.01, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 0.01,
      isDimensionless: true, scaledDimensionless: true, displayTex: "%"
    };
  }
  if (COMPOUND_UNITS[uName]) {
    const c = COMPOUND_UNITS[uName];
    const factor = c.factor || 1.0;
    const atoms = { ...c.atoms };
    let scale = factor;
    const dimMap = {};
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      if (b) {
        dimMap[b.dim] = (dimMap[b.dim] || 0) + p;
        scale *= Math.pow(b.scale, p);
      }
    }
    return { factor, atoms, dimMap, scale, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: c.displayTex, substituteTex: c.substituteTex };
  }
  if (ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName]) {
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName];
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: { [uName]: 1 }, dimMap: { [b.dim]: 1 }, scale: b.scale, offset: b.offset || 0, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: b.tex };
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: false, isUnknown: true, rawUnit: uName };
}

export function isDimEqual(aMap, bMap) {
  const all = new Set([...Object.keys(aMap || {}), ...Object.keys(bMap || {})]);
  for (const d of all) if ((aMap?.[d] || 0) !== (bMap?.[d] || 0)) return false;
  return true;
}

const UNIT_TEX_ALIASES = Object.freeze({ "%": "%", "°": "^{\\circ}" });

export function unitToTex(unitName = "") {
  const cleaned = String(unitName || "").trim();
  if (!cleaned || cleaned === "dimless" || cleaned === "ratio") return "";
  if (UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned]) return UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned];

  const [numPart = "", denPart = ""] = cleaned.split("/");
  const parse = part => part.split(/[·* ]+/).filter(Boolean).map(tok => {
    const m = tok.match(/^([A-Za-z°%_]+)(?:\^(-?\d+))?$/);
    if (!m) return tok === "%" ? "%" : `\\text{${tok}}`;
    const base = UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[m[1]] || (m[1] === "%" ? "%" : `\\text{${m[1]}}`);
    return m[2] ? `${base}^{${m[2]}}` : base;
  });

  const num = parse(numPart);
  const den = parse(denPart);
  if (!den.length) return num.join("\\,");
  if (!num.length) return `\\cfrac{1}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
  return `\\cfrac{${num.join("\\cdot ")}}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value from internal coherent base to target unit.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function formatValueInUnit(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;
  if (!scale) return value;

  // Temperature affine conversions from coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_C = T_K - 273.15
      // For °F: T_F = (T_K / (5/9)) - 459.67
      return (value / scale) - b.offset;
    }
  }

  return value / scale;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value in given unit back to internal coherent base.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function valueToCoherentBase(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;

  // Temperature affine conversions to coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_K = (T_C + 273.15) * 1.0
      // For °F: T_K = (T_F + 459.67) * (5/9)
      return (value + b.offset) * scale;
    }
  }

  return value * scale;
}

```javascript
/**
 * src/core/dimensions.js
 * Physical dimensions, KaTeX cancellation formatters, and universal number formatting.
 */

export const DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({
  LENGTH: "Length",
  FORCE: "Force",
  TORQUE: "Torque",
  POWER: "Power",
  PRESSURE: "Pressure / Stress",
  STRESS: "Pressure / Stress",
  VELOCITY: "Linear Velocity",
  ROT_SPEED: "Rotational Speed",
  PITCH: "Tooth Pitch",
  ANGLE: "Angle",
  COUNT: "Count",
  ENERGY: "Energy / Work",
  SPECIFIC_ENERGY: "Specific Energy / Enthalpy",
  MASS: "Mass",
  MASS_FLOW: "Mass Flow Rate",
  VOLUME: "Volume",
  VOLUME_FLOW: "Volume Flow Rate",
  SPECIFIC_CONSUMPTION: "Specific Fuel Consumption",
  TEMPERATURE: "Temperature",
  TEMPERATURE_DIFF: "Temperature Difference",
  PERCENTAGE: "Percentage",
  DIMENSIONLESS: "Dimensionless"
});

/**
 * Universal Number Formatter:
 * Enforces up to 6 significant figures, with a strict maximum of 3 decimal places.
 * Formats numbers with thousand separators. Trims trailing zeroes to the right of the decimal.
 */
export function fmtSigFig(n, sigFigs = 6) {
  if (n === undefined || n === null) return "";
  const num = typeof n === "number" ? n : Number(n);
  if (isNaN(num)) return "";

  const addSeparators = (valStr) => {
    const parts = valStr.split(".");
    parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ",");
    return parts.join(".");
  };

  if (Number.isInteger(num)) return addSeparators(num.toString());

  // 1. Apply significant figures precision first
  let formatted = parseFloat(num.toPrecision(sigFigs));

  // 2. Enforce maximum of 3 decimal places across the entire repository
  if (Math.abs(formatted) < 1e-4) return "0";

  const str = formatted.toString();
  if (str.includes(".")) {
    const parts = str.split(".");
    if (parts[1].length > 3) {
      formatted = Number(formatted.toFixed(3));
    }
  }

  return addSeparators(formatted.toString());
}

export function fmt(n) {
  return fmtSigFig(n, 6);
}

/**
 * Given-value formatter. Measured givens round to 6 s.f.; constants bypass
 * rounding entirely and print their tabulated academic form (meta.display),
 * or full precision when no tabulated form is declared.
 * @param {number} value
 * @param {object} meta varsDb / CONSTANTS_DB entry for the variable
 */
export function formatGivenValue(value, meta = {}) {
  if (meta.isConstant) {
    if (typeof meta.display === "number") return String(meta.display);
    if (typeof meta.display === "string") return meta.display;
    return String(value);
  }
  return fmtSigFig(value, 6);
}

export function cancelUnit(unitStr) {
  if (!unitStr) return "";
  if (unitStr === "%") return "\\cancel{\\%}";
  return `\\cancel{\\text{${unitStr}}}`;
}

export function textUnit(unitStr) {
  if (!unitStr) return "";
  if (unitStr === "%") return "\\%";
  if (unitStr.startsWith("°")) return unitStr;
  return `\\text{${unitStr}}`;
}

export function targetLabel(name, token, unit) {
  if (!unit || unit === "dimless" || unit === "ratio") {
    return `${name} (${token})`;
  }
  if (unit === "deg" || unit === "°") {
    return `${name} (${token}) in °`;
  }
  if (unit === "%") {
    return `${name} (${token}) in %`;
  }
  return `${name} (${token}) in ${unit}`;
}

/**
 * src/cas/units/definitions.js
 * Comprehensive engineering unit registry with:
 * - Exact affine temperature offset transformations for °C and °F
 * - Support for vector temperature differences (bypassing affine origin offsets)
 * - Scaled percentage dimension (1% = 0.01)
 * - Single source for unit TeX (unitToTex) and value formatting
 */

export const BASE_DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({
  L: "Length", T: "Time", F: "Force", M: "Mass", Th: "Temperature", C: "Count", E: "Energy"
});

export const ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS = Object.freeze({

  "m/s":      { atoms: { m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "m/min":    { factor: 1 / 60, atoms: { m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "ft/min":   { atoms: { ft: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "ft/s":     { atoms: { ft: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}}{\\text{s}}" },

  "teeth/in": { atoms: { teeth: 1, in: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{teeth}}{\\text{in}}" },
  rpm:        { atoms: { min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{rpm}" },
  "rev/s":    { atoms: { s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{rev}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "rev/min":  { atoms: { min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{rev}}{\\text{min}}" },

  "kJ/(kg*K)":{ factor: 1000, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, kg: -1, K: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{K}}" },
  "kJ/kg-K":  { factor: 1000, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, kg: -1, K: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{K}}" },
  "Btu/(lb*F)":{ atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1, "°F": -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}\\cdot^{\\circ}\\text{F}}" },
  "Btu/lb-F":  { atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1, "°F": -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}\\cdot^{\\circ}\\text{F}}" }
});

export function getUnitAtomBag(uName) {
  if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") {
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: true };
  }
  if (uName === "%") {
    return {
      factor: 0.01, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 0.01,
      isDimensionless: true, scaledDimensionless: true, displayTex: "\\%"
    };
  }
  if (COMPOUND_UNITS[uName]) {
    const c = COMPOUND_UNITS[uName];
    const factor = c.factor || 1.0;
    const atoms = { ...c.atoms };
    let scale = factor;
    const dimMap = {};
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      if (b) {
        dimMap[b.dim] = (dimMap[b.dim] || 0) + p;
        scale *= Math.pow(b.scale, p);
      }
    }
    return { factor, atoms, dimMap, scale, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: c.displayTex, substituteTex: c.substituteTex };
  }
  if (ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName]) {
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName];
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: { [uName]: 1 }, dimMap: { [b.dim]: 1 }, scale: b.scale, offset: b.offset || 0, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: b.tex };
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: false, isUnknown: true, rawUnit: uName };
}

export function isDimEqual(aMap, bMap) {
  const all = new Set([...Object.keys(aMap || {}), ...Object.keys(bMap || {})]);
  for (const d of all) if ((aMap?.[d] || 0) !== (bMap?.[d] || 0)) return false;
  return true;
}

const UNIT_TEX_ALIASES = Object.freeze({
  "%": "\\%",
  "°": "^{\\circ}",
  "°C": "^{\\circ}\\text{C}",
  "°F": "^{\\circ}\\text{F}",
  "°R": "^{\\circ}\\text{R}"
});

export function unitToTex(unitName = "") {
  const cleaned = String(unitName || "").trim();
  if (!cleaned || cleaned === "dimless" || cleaned === "ratio") return "";
  if (UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned]) return UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned];

  const [numPart = "", denPart = ""] = cleaned.split("/");
  const parse = part => part.split(/[·* ]+/).filter(Boolean).map(tok => {
    const m = tok.match(/^([A-Za-z°%_]+)(?:\^(-?\d+))?$/);
    if (!m) return tok === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${tok}}`;
    const base = UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[m[1]] || (m[1] === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${m[1]}}`);
    return m[2] ? `${base}^{${m[2]}}` : base;
  });

  const num = parse(numPart);
  const den = parse(denPart);
  if (!den.length) return num.join("\\,");
  if (!num.length) return `\\cfrac{1}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
  return `\\cfrac{${num.join("\\cdot ")}}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value from internal coherent base to target unit.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function formatValueInUnit(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;
  if (!scale) return value;

  // Temperature affine conversions from coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_C = T_K - 273.15
      // For °F: T_F = (T_K / (5/9)) - 459.67
      return (value / scale) - b.offset;
    }
  }

  return value / scale;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value in given unit back to internal coherent base.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function valueToCoherentBase(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;

  // Temperature affine conversions to coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_K = (T_C + 273.15) * 1.0
      // For °F: T_K = (T_F + 459.67) * (5/9)
      return (value + b.offset) * scale;
    }
  }

  return value * scale;
}

/**

 * - Support for vector temperature differences (bypassing affine origin offsets)

 * - Single source for unit TeX (unitToTex) and value formatting
 */

export const BASE_DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({

export function getUnitAtomBag(uName) {
  if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") {
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: true };
  }
  if (uName === "%") {
    return {
      factor: 0.01, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 0.01,
      isDimensionless: true, scaledDimensionless: true, displayTex: "\\%"
    };
  }
  if (COMPOUND_UNITS[uName]) {
    const c = COMPOUND_UNITS[uName];
    const factor = c.factor || 1.0;
    const atoms = { ...c.atoms };
    let scale = factor;
    const dimMap = {};
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      if (b) {
        dimMap[b.dim] = (dimMap[b.dim] || 0) + p;
        scale *= Math.pow(b.scale, p);
      }
    }
    return { factor, atoms, dimMap, scale, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: c.displayTex, substituteTex: c.substituteTex };
  }
  if (ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName]) {
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName];
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: { [uName]: 1 }, dimMap: { [b.dim]: 1 }, scale: b.scale, offset: b.offset || 0, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: b.tex };
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: false, isUnknown: true, rawUnit: uName };
}

export function isDimEqual(aMap, bMap) {
  const all = new Set([...Object.keys(aMap || {}), ...Object.keys(bMap || {})]);
  for (const d of all) if ((aMap?.[d] || 0) !== (bMap?.[d] || 0)) return false;
  return true;
}

const UNIT_TEX_ALIASES = Object.freeze({
  "%": "\\%",
  "°": "^{\\circ}",
  "°C": "^{\\circ}\\text{C}",
  "°F": "^{\\circ}\\text{F}",
  "°R": "^{\\circ}\\text{R}"
});

export function unitToTex(unitName = "") {
  const cleaned = String(unitName || "").trim();
  if (!cleaned || cleaned === "dimless" || cleaned === "ratio") return "";
  if (UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned]) return UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned];

  const [numPart = "", denPart = ""] = cleaned.split("/");
  const parse = part => part.split(/[·* ]+/).filter(Boolean).map(tok => {
    const m = tok.match(/^([A-Za-z°%_]+)(?:\^(-?\d+))?$/);
    if (!m) return tok === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${tok}}`;
    const base = UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[m[1]] || (m[1] === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${m[1]}}`);
    return m[2] ? `${base}^{${m[2]}}` : base;
  });

  const num = parse(numPart);
  const den = parse(denPart);
  if (!den.length) return num.join("\\,");
  if (!num.length) return `\\cfrac{1}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
  return `\\cfrac{${num.join("\\cdot ")}}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value from internal coherent base to target unit.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function formatValueInUnit(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;
  if (!scale) return value;

  // Temperature affine conversions from coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_C = T_K - 273.15
      // For °F: T_F = (T_K / (5/9)) - 459.67
      return (value / scale) - b.offset;
    }
  }

  return value / scale;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value in given unit back to internal coherent base.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function valueToCoherentBase(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;

  // Temperature affine conversions to coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_K = (T_C + 273.15) * 1.0
      // For °F: T_K = (T_F + 459.67) * (5/9)
      return (value + b.offset) * scale;
    }
  }

  return value * scale;
}

/**
 * src/cas/units/analysis.js
 * Universal dimensional analysis:
 * - Cancel-first atomic cancellation across expression token bags
 * - Single global scale bracket collapse
 * - Universal projection for empirical relations: converts formula output unit into targetUnit
 * - Scaled dimensionless units (%, silentScale) fold into the numeric factor
 * - All TeX output passes through unitToTex
 */

import { parseMathAST, evalAST, getAstIdentifiers } from "../ast.js";
import { resolveCanonicalKey, isReservedMathToken } from "../algebra.js";
import { fmtSigFig } from "../../core/dimensions.js";
import { ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS, getUnitAtomBag, isDimEqual, unitToTex } from "./definitions.js";

export function evalExprUnitAST(node, S, varsDb) {
  if (!node) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "num") return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "id") {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(node.name, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[node.name] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[node.name]?.unit || "dimless";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    const atoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      atoms.push({
        token: node.name, canonicalKey: cKey, unitName: uName, atom: aName,
        dim: b?.dim || "custom", scale: b?.scale || 1.0, power: p, tex: b?.tex || `\\text{${aName}}`
      });
    }
    return { factor: bag.factor, atoms };
  }
  if (node.type === "group") return evalExprUnitAST(node.expr, S, varsDb);

  if (node.type === "call") {
    if (["sin", "cos", "tan"].includes(node.fn)) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
    if (["atan", "acos", "asin"].includes(node.fn)) {
      return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [{ token: node.fn, canonicalKey: node.fn, unitName: "rad", atom: "rad", dim: "C", scale: 1.0, power: 1, tex: "\\text{rad}" }] };
    }
    if (node.fn === "sqrt") {
      const inner = evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
      return { factor: Math.sqrt(inner.factor), atoms: inner.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power / 2 })) };
    }
    return evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
  }

  if (node.type === "binary") {
    const left = evalExprUnitAST(node.left, S, varsDb);
    const right = evalExprUnitAST(node.right, S, varsDb);
    if (node.op === "*") return { factor: left.factor * right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms] };
    if (node.op === "/") return { factor: left.factor / right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: -a.power }))] };
    if (node.op === "+" || node.op === "-") return left;
    if (node.op === "^") {
      const pNum = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      return { factor: Math.pow(left.factor, pNum), atoms: left.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power * pNum })) };
    }
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
}

function collectTokenExponents(node, sign = 1, acc = {}) {
  if (!node) return acc;
  if (node.type === "group") return collectTokenExponents(node.expr, sign, acc);
  if (node.type === "id") {
    acc[node.name] = (acc[node.name] || 0) + sign;
    return acc;
  }
  if (node.type === "binary") {
    if (node.op === "*") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "/") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, -sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "^") {
      const p = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign * p, acc);
    }
  }
  return acc;
}

export function solveUnitAlgebra(exprOrAST, S, targetVar, targetUnit, varsDb = {}, requiredUnits = null) {
  const ast = (typeof exprOrAST === "string") ? parseMathAST(exprOrAST) : exprOrAST;
  const tokenConversions = {};
  const preConversions = [];
  const tokenMarkup = {};

  // 1. Handling relations with explicit requiredUnits contracts
  if (requiredUnits && typeof requiredUnits === "object") {
    const varTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast).filter(t => !isReservedMathToken(t))));
    varTokens.forEach(tok => {
      const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
      const reqU = requiredUnits[cKey] || requiredUnits[tok];
      if (!reqU) return;
      const currU = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";

      if (currU === "°C" && (reqU === "K" || reqU === "degK")) {
        const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
        preConversions.push({
          token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
          conv: { factor: 1.0, latex: `(${rawVal} + 273.15)\\,\\text{K}` },
          rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal + 273.15
        });
        return;
      }
      if (currU && reqU && currU !== reqU && currU !== "deg" && reqU !== "°") {
        const currBag = getUnitAtomBag(currU), reqBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqU);
        if (isDimEqual(currBag.dimMap, reqBag.dimMap)) {
          const ratio = currBag.scale / reqBag.scale;
          const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
          preConversions.push({
            token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
            conv: { factor: ratio, latex: `\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqU)}}{\\cancel{${unitToTex(currU)}}}` },
            rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal * ratio
          });
        }
      }
    });

    // Universal target-side conversion for all empirical relations:
    // Compares formula's output unit (reqTargetU) with requested unit (targetUnit)
    const cTarget = resolveCanonicalKey(targetVar, varsDb);
    const reqTargetU = requiredUnits[cTarget] || requiredUnits[targetVar];
    let numericConversionFactor = 1.0;
    let globalConversionTex = "";

    if (reqTargetU && targetUnit && reqTargetU !== targetUnit) {
      const reqTargetBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqTargetU);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      if (isDimEqual(reqTargetBag.dimMap, targetBag.dimMap)) {
        numericConversionFactor = reqTargetBag.scale / targetBag.scale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          const ratio = numericConversionFactor;
          if (ratio < 1) {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{1\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{${fmtSigFig(1 / ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          } else {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{1\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (!reqTargetU && targetUnit) {
      const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
      exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });
      const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
      if (targetScale > 0) {
        numericConversionFactor = exprScale / targetScale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(numericConversionFactor, 6)}\\right]`;
        }
      }
    }

    return { tokenMarkup, conversionFactors: [], conversionFactorTex: "", numericConversionFactor, globalConversionTex, preConversions, tokenConversions };
  }

  // 2. Pure physics dimensional analysis
  const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
  const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);

  let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
  exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });

  const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
  const numericConversionFactor = targetScale > 0 ? (exprScale / targetScale) : 1.0;

  let silentExpr = 1.0;
  const exps = collectTokenExponents(ast);
  for (const [tok, exp] of Object.entries(exps)) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) silentExpr *= Math.pow(bag.scale || 1, exp);
  }
  const silentTarget = targetBag.scaledDimensionless ? (targetBag.scale || 1) : 1;
  const silentPart = silentExpr / silentTarget;
  const bracketFactor = silentPart !== 0 ? numericConversionFactor / silentPart : numericConversionFactor;
  const globalConversionTex = (Math.abs(bracketFactor - 1) > 1e-12)
    ? `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(bracketFactor, 6)}\\right]`
    : "";

  const atomList = exprAnalysis.atoms.map((a, idx) => ({ ...a, id: idx, canceled: false }));
  for (let i = 0; i < atomList.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < atomList.length; j++) {
      const a1 = atomList[i], a2 = atomList[j];
      if (a1.atom === a2.atom && a1.power * a2.power < 0 && !a1.canceled && !a2.canceled) {
        a1.canceled = true; a2.canceled = true;
      }
    }
  }

  const allExprTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast)));
  for (const tok of allExprTokens) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") continue;

    if (uName === "deg" || uName === "°") {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = "^{\\circ}";
      continue;
    }

    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (uName === targetUnit) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (bag.substituteTex) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.substituteTex;
      continue;
    }

    const formatAtom = (atomName, p) => {
      const isCanceled = atomList.some(a => a.token === tok && a.atom === atomName && a.canceled);
      const expStr = Math.abs(p) > 1 ? `^{${Math.abs(p)}}` : "";
      const atomTex = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName]?.tex || `\\text{${atomName}}`;
      return isCanceled ? `\\cancel{${atomTex}}${expStr}` : `${atomTex}${expStr}`;
    };

    const numAtoms = [];
    const denAtoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      if (p > 0) numAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
      else if (p < 0) denAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
    }

    const numStr = numAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    const denStr = denAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    tokenMarkup[tok] = denStr ? `\\cfrac{${numStr || "1"}}{${denStr}}` : numStr;
  }

  return {
    tokenMarkup,
    conversionFactors: [],
    conversionFactorTex: "",
    numericConversionFactor,
    globalConversionTex,
    preConversions,
    tokenConversions
  };
}

/**
 * src/cas/units/definitions.js
 * Comprehensive engineering unit registry with:
 * - Exact affine temperature offset transformations for °C and °F
 * - Support for vector temperature differences (bypassing affine origin offsets)
 * - Scaled percentage dimension (1% = 0.01)
 * - Single source for unit TeX (unitToTex) and value formatting
 */

export const BASE_DIMENSIONS = Object.freeze({
  L: "Length", T: "Time", F: "Force", M: "Mass", Th: "Temperature", C: "Count", E: "Energy"
});

export const ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS = Object.freeze({
  m:     { dim: "L", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{m}" },
  mm:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.001, tex: "\\text{mm}" },
  cm:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.01, tex: "\\text{cm}" },
  in:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.0254, tex: "\\text{in}" },
  ft:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.3048, tex: "\\text{ft}" },
  yd:    { dim: "L", scale: 0.9144, tex: "\\text{yd}" },
  s:     { dim: "T", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{s}" },
  min:   { dim: "T", scale: 60.0, tex: "\\text{min}" },
  h:     { dim: "T", scale: 3600.0, tex: "\\text{h}" },
  hr:    { dim: "T", scale: 3600.0, tex: "\\text{h}" },
  N:     { dim: "F", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{N}" },
  kN:    { dim: "F", scale: 1000.0, tex: "\\text{kN}" },
  lb:    { dim: "F", scale: 4.4482216, tex: "\\text{lb}" },
  kip:   { dim: "F", scale: 4448.2216, tex: "\\text{kip}" },
  kg:    { dim: "M", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{kg}" },
  g:     { dim: "M", scale: 0.001, tex: "\\text{g}" },
  lb_m:  { dim: "M", scale: 0.45359237, tex: "\\text{lb}_m" },
  K:     { dim: "Th", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{K}" },
  "°C":  { dim: "Th", scale: 1.0, offset: 273.15, tex: "°\\text{C}" },
  "°F":  { dim: "Th", scale: 5 / 9, offset: 459.67, tex: "°\\text{F}" },
  "°R":  { dim: "Th", scale: 5 / 9, tex: "°\\text{R}" },
  Btu:   { dim: "E", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{Btu}" },
  teeth: { dim: "C", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{teeth}" },
  rad:   { dim: "C", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{rad}" },
  rev:   { dim: "C", scale: 1.0, tex: "\\text{rev}" },
  "°":   { dim: "C", scale: Math.PI / 180, tex: "°" },
  deg:   { dim: "C", scale: Math.PI / 180, tex: "°" }
});

export const COMPOUND_UNITS = Object.freeze({
  psi:        { atoms: { lb: 1, in: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{psi}" },
  psf:        { atoms: { lb: 1, ft: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{psf}" },
  ksi:        { factor: 6894757.29, atoms: { N: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{ksi}" },
  MPa:        { atoms: { N: 1, mm: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{MPa}", substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}}{\\text{mm}^2}" },
  Pa:         { atoms: { N: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{Pa}", substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}}{\\text{m}^2}" },
  kPa:        { atoms: { kN: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{kPa}", substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kN}}{\\text{m}^2}" },
  bar:        { factor: 100000, atoms: { N: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\text{bar}" },

  "N/m^2":    { atoms: { N: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}}{\\text{m}^2}" },
  "kN/m^2":   { atoms: { kN: 1, m: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kN}}{\\text{m}^2}" },
  "N/mm^2":   { atoms: { N: 1, mm: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}}{\\text{mm}^2}" },
  "lb/in^2":  { atoms: { lb: 1, in: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{in}^2}" },
  "lb/ft^2":  { atoms: { lb: 1, ft: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{ft}^2}" },

  "N-m":      { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1 }, displayTex: "\\text{N}\\cdot\\text{m}" },
  "N*m":      { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1 }, displayTex: "\\text{N}\\cdot\\text{m}" },
  "kN-m":     { atoms: { kN: 1, m: 1 }, displayTex: "\\text{kN}\\cdot\\text{m}" },

  W:          { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{W}", substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{J}}{\\text{s}}" },
  kW:         { atoms: { kN: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{kW}", substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{s}}" },
  MW:         { factor: 1e6, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{MW}" },
  hp:         { factor: 33000, atoms: { ft: 1, lb: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{hp}" },

  "J/s":          { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{J}}{\\text{s}}" },

  "N-m/s":        { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}\\cdot\\text{m}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "N*m/s":        { atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{N}\\cdot\\text{m}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "kN-m/s":       { atoms: { kN: 1, m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kN}\\cdot\\text{m}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "kg*m^2/s^3":   { atoms: { kg: 1, m: 2, s: -3 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{m}^2}{\\text{s}^3}" },
  "kg-m^2/s^3":   { atoms: { kg: 1, m: 2, s: -3 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{m}^2}{\\text{s}^3}" },
  "ft-lb/s":      { atoms: { ft: 1, lb: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}\\cdot\\text{lb}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "ft-lb/min":    { atoms: { ft: 1, lb: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}\\cdot\\text{lb}}{\\text{min}}" },

  "kg*m/s^2":     { atoms: { kg: 1, m: 1, s: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{m}}{\\text{s}^2}" },
  "kg-m/s^2":     { atoms: { kg: 1, m: 1, s: -2 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{m}}{\\text{s}^2}" },

  "m^2":      { atoms: { m: 2 }, displayTex: "\\text{m}^2" },
  "in^2":     { atoms: { in: 2 }, displayTex: "\\text{in}^2" },

  "m^3/h":    { atoms: { m: 3, h: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}^3}{\\text{h}}" },
  "L/h":      { factor: 0.001 / 3600, atoms: { m: 3, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{L}}{\\text{h}}" },
  cfm:        { factor: 0.3048 ** 3 / 60, atoms: { m: 3, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{cfm}" },

  "kg/m^3":   { atoms: { kg: 1, m: -3 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}}{\\text{m}^3}" },
  "m^3/kg":   { atoms: { m: 3, kg: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}^3}{\\text{kg}}" },
  "lb/ft^3":  { atoms: { lb_m: 1, ft: -3 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{ft}^3}" },
  "ft^3/lb":  { atoms: { ft: 3, lb_m: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}^3}{\\text{lb}}" },

  "kJ/kg":    { atoms: { kN: 1, m: 1, kg: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}}" },
  "Btu/lb":   { atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}}" },
  "Btu/min":  { atoms: { Btu: 1, min: -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "Btu/h":    { atoms: { Btu: 1, h: -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{h}}" },
  "Btu/hr":   { atoms: { Btu: 1, h: -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{h}}" },

  kWh:        { factor: 3.6e6, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1 }, displayTex: "\\text{kWh}" },
  "kg/kWh":   { factor: 1 / 3.6e6, atoms: { kg: 1, N: -1, m: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}}{\\text{kWh}}" },
  "g/kWh":    { factor: 0.001 / 3.6e6, atoms: { g: 1, N: -1, m: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{g}}{\\text{kWh}}" },
  "kg/h":     { factor: 1 / 3600, atoms: { kg: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}}{\\text{h}}" },
  "lb/hp-h":  { factor: 0.45359237 / (745.69987 * 3600), atoms: { kg: 1, N: -1, m: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{hp}\\cdot\\text{h}}" },
  "lb/hp-hr": { factor: 0.45359237 / (745.69987 * 3600), atoms: { kg: 1, N: -1, m: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{hp}\\cdot\\text{h}}" },

  "m/s":      { atoms: { m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "m/min":    { factor: 1 / 60, atoms: { m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{m}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "ft/min":   { atoms: { ft: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "ft/s":     { atoms: { ft: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{ft}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "kg/s":     { atoms: { kg: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "g/s":      { atoms: { g: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{g}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "kg/min":   { atoms: { kg: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kg}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "lb/min":   { atoms: { lb_m: 1, min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "lb/s":     { atoms: { lb_m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "lb/h":     { factor: 1 / 3600, atoms: { lb_m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{h}}" },
  "lb/hr":    { factor: 1 / 3600, atoms: { lb_m: 1, s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{lb}}{\\text{h}}" },

  "teeth/in": { atoms: { teeth: 1, in: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{teeth}}{\\text{in}}" },
  rpm:        { atoms: { min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{rpm}" },
  "rev/s":    { atoms: { s: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{rev}}{\\text{s}}" },
  "rev/min":  { atoms: { min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{rev}}{\\text{min}}" },
  "strokes/min": { atoms: { min: -1 }, displayTex: "\\text{strokes/min}" },

  "kJ/(kg*K)":{ factor: 1000, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, kg: -1, K: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{K}}" },
  "kJ/kg-K":  { factor: 1000, atoms: { N: 1, m: 1, kg: -1, K: -1 }, displayTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{kJ}}{\\text{kg}\\cdot\\text{K}}" },
  "Btu/(lb*F)":{ atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1, "°F": -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}\\cdot°\\text{F}}" },
  "Btu/lb-F":  { atoms: { Btu: 1, lb_m: -1, "°F": -1 }, substituteTex: "\\cfrac{\\text{Btu}}{\\text{lb}\\cdot°\\text{F}}" }
});

export function getUnitAtomBag(uName) {
  if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") {
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: true };
  }
  if (uName === "%") {
    return {
      factor: 0.01, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 0.01,
      isDimensionless: true, scaledDimensionless: true, displayTex: "\\%"
    };
  }
  if (COMPOUND_UNITS[uName]) {
    const c = COMPOUND_UNITS[uName];
    const factor = c.factor || 1.0;
    const atoms = { ...c.atoms };
    let scale = factor;
    const dimMap = {};
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      if (b) {
        dimMap[b.dim] = (dimMap[b.dim] || 0) + p;
        scale *= Math.pow(b.scale, p);
      }
    }
    return { factor, atoms, dimMap, scale, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: c.displayTex, substituteTex: c.substituteTex };
  }
  if (ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName]) {
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[uName];
    return { factor: 1.0, atoms: { [uName]: 1 }, dimMap: { [b.dim]: 1 }, scale: b.scale, offset: b.offset || 0, isDimensionless: false, displayTex: b.tex };
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: {}, dimMap: {}, scale: 1.0, isDimensionless: false, isUnknown: true, rawUnit: uName };
}

export function isDimEqual(aMap, bMap) {
  const all = new Set([...Object.keys(aMap || {}), ...Object.keys(bMap || {})]);
  for (const d of all) if ((aMap?.[d] || 0) !== (bMap?.[d] || 0)) return false;
  return true;
}

const UNIT_TEX_ALIASES = Object.freeze({
  "%": "\\%",
  "°": "°",
  "°C": "°\\text{C}",
  "°F": "°\\text{F}",
  "°R": "°\\text{R}"
});

export function unitToTex(unitName = "") {
  const cleaned = String(unitName || "").trim();
  if (!cleaned || cleaned === "dimless" || cleaned === "ratio") return "";
  if (UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned]) return UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[cleaned];

  const [numPart = "", denPart = ""] = cleaned.split("/");
  const parse = part => part.split(/[·* ]+/).filter(Boolean).map(tok => {
    const m = tok.match(/^([A-Za-z°%_]+)(?:\^(-?\d+))?$/);
    if (!m) return tok === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${tok}}`;
    const base = UNIT_TEX_ALIASES[m[1]] || (m[1] === "%" ? "\\%" : `\\text{${m[1]}}`);
    return m[2] ? `${base}^{${m[2]}}` : base;
  });

  const num = parse(numPart);
  const den = parse(denPart);
  if (!den.length) return num.join("\\,");
  if (!num.length) return `\\cfrac{1}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
  return `\\cfrac{${num.join("\\cdot ")}}{${den.join("\\cdot ")}}`;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value from internal coherent base to target unit.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function formatValueInUnit(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;
  if (!scale) return value;

  // Temperature affine conversions from coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_C = T_K - 273.15
      // For °F: T_F = (T_K / (5/9)) - 459.67
      return (value / scale) - b.offset;
    }
  }

  return value / scale;
}

/**
 * Converts numeric value in given unit back to internal coherent base.
 * If isDifference is true (for temperature intervals/differences), affine origin
 * offsets are bypassed and only the linear scale factor is applied.
 */
export function valueToCoherentBase(value, unitName = "", isDifference = false) {
  const bag = getUnitAtomBag(unitName);
  const scale = bag?.scale || 1.0;

  // Temperature affine conversions to coherent Kelvin base
  if (!isDifference && bag.atoms && Object.keys(bag.atoms).length === 1 && bag.dimMap?.Th === 1) {
    const atomName = Object.keys(bag.atoms)[0];
    const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName];
    if (b && b.offset) {
      // For °C: T_K = (T_C + 273.15) * 1.0
      // For °F: T_K = (T_F + 459.67) * (5/9)
      return (value + b.offset) * scale;
    }
  }

  return value * scale;
}

```
/**
 * src/cas/units/analysis.js
 * Universal dimensional analysis:
 * - Cancel-first atomic cancellation across expression token bags
 * - Single global scale bracket collapse
 * - Universal projection for empirical relations: converts formula output unit into targetUnit
 * - Scaled dimensionless units (%, silentScale) fold into the numeric factor
 * - All TeX output passes through unitToTex
 */

import { parseMathAST, evalAST, getAstIdentifiers } from "../ast.js";
import { resolveCanonicalKey, isReservedMathToken } from "../algebra.js";
import { fmtSigFig } from "../../core/dimensions.js";
import { ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS, getUnitAtomBag, isDimEqual, unitToTex } from "./definitions.js";

export function evalExprUnitAST(node, S, varsDb) {
  if (!node) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "num") return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "id") {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(node.name, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[node.name] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[node.name]?.unit || "dimless";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    const atoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      atoms.push({
        token: node.name, canonicalKey: cKey, unitName: uName, atom: aName,
        dim: b?.dim || "custom", scale: b?.scale || 1.0, power: p, tex: b?.tex || `\\text{${aName}}`
      });
    }
    return { factor: bag.factor, atoms };
  }
  if (node.type === "group") return evalExprUnitAST(node.expr, S, varsDb);

  if (node.type === "call") {
    if (["sin", "cos", "tan"].includes(node.fn)) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
    if (["atan", "acos", "asin"].includes(node.fn)) {
      return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [{ token: node.fn, canonicalKey: node.fn, unitName: "rad", atom: "rad", dim: "C", scale: 1.0, power: 1, tex: "\\text{rad}" }] };
    }
    if (node.fn === "sqrt") {
      const inner = evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
      return { factor: Math.sqrt(inner.factor), atoms: inner.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power / 2 })) };
    }
    return evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
  }

  if (node.type === "binary") {
    const left = evalExprUnitAST(node.left, S, varsDb);
    const right = evalExprUnitAST(node.right, S, varsDb);
    if (node.op === "*") return { factor: left.factor * right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms] };
    if (node.op === "/") return { factor: left.factor / right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: -a.power }))] };
    if (node.op === "+" || node.op === "-") return left;
    if (node.op === "^") {
      const pNum = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      return { factor: Math.pow(left.factor, pNum), atoms: left.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power * pNum })) };
    }
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
}

function collectTokenExponents(node, sign = 1, acc = {}) {
  if (!node) return acc;
  if (node.type === "group") return collectTokenExponents(node.expr, sign, acc);
  if (node.type === "id") {
    acc[node.name] = (acc[node.name] || 0) + sign;
    return acc;
  }
  if (node.type === "binary") {
    if (node.op === "*") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "/") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, -sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "^") {
      const p = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign * p, acc);
    }
  }
  return acc;
}

export function solveUnitAlgebra(exprOrAST, S, targetVar, targetUnit, varsDb = {}, requiredUnits = null) {
  const ast = (typeof exprOrAST === "string") ? parseMathAST(exprOrAST) : exprOrAST;
  const tokenConversions = {};
  const preConversions = [];
  const tokenMarkup = {};

  // 1. Handling relations with explicit requiredUnits contracts
  if (requiredUnits && typeof requiredUnits === "object") {
    const varTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast).filter(t => !isReservedMathToken(t))));
    varTokens.forEach(tok => {
      const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
      const reqU = requiredUnits[cKey] || requiredUnits[tok];
      if (!reqU) return;
      const currU = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";

      if (currU === "°C" && (reqU === "K" || reqU === "degK")) {
        const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
        preConversions.push({
          token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
          conv: { factor: 1.0, latex: `(${rawVal} + 273.15)\\,\\text{K}` },
          rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal + 273.15
        });
        return;
      }
      if (currU && reqU && currU !== reqU && currU !== "deg" && reqU !== "°") {
        const currBag = getUnitAtomBag(currU), reqBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqU);
        if (isDimEqual(currBag.dimMap, reqBag.dimMap)) {
          const ratio = currBag.scale / reqBag.scale;
          const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
          preConversions.push({
            token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
            conv: { factor: ratio, latex: `\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqU)}}{\\cancel{${unitToTex(currU)}}}` },
            rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal * ratio
          });
        }
      }
    });

    // Universal target-side conversion for all empirical relations:
    // Compares formula's output unit (reqTargetU) with requested unit (targetUnit)
    const cTarget = resolveCanonicalKey(targetVar, varsDb);
    const reqTargetU = requiredUnits[cTarget] || requiredUnits[targetVar];
    let numericConversionFactor = 1.0;
    let globalConversionTex = "";

    if (reqTargetU && targetUnit && reqTargetU !== targetUnit) {
      const reqTargetBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqTargetU);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      if (isDimEqual(reqTargetBag.dimMap, targetBag.dimMap)) {
        numericConversionFactor = reqTargetBag.scale / targetBag.scale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          const ratio = numericConversionFactor;
          if (ratio < 1) {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{1\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{${fmtSigFig(1 / ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          } else {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{1\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (!reqTargetU && targetUnit) {
      const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
      exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });
      const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
      if (targetScale > 0) {
        numericConversionFactor = exprScale / targetScale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(numericConversionFactor, 6)}\\right]`;
        }
      }
    }

    return { tokenMarkup, conversionFactors: [], conversionFactorTex: "", numericConversionFactor, globalConversionTex, preConversions, tokenConversions };
  }

  // 2. Pure physics dimensional analysis
  const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
  const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);

  let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
  exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });

  const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
  const numericConversionFactor = targetScale > 0 ? (exprScale / targetScale) : 1.0;

  let silentExpr = 1.0;
  const exps = collectTokenExponents(ast);
  for (const [tok, exp] of Object.entries(exps)) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) silentExpr *= Math.pow(bag.scale || 1, exp);
  }
  const silentTarget = targetBag.scaledDimensionless ? (targetBag.scale || 1) : 1;
  const silentPart = silentExpr / silentTarget;
  const bracketFactor = silentPart !== 0 ? numericConversionFactor / silentPart : numericConversionFactor;
  const globalConversionTex = (Math.abs(bracketFactor - 1) > 1e-12)
    ? `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(bracketFactor, 6)}\\right]`
    : "";

  const atomList = exprAnalysis.atoms.map((a, idx) => ({ ...a, id: idx, canceled: false }));
  for (let i = 0; i < atomList.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < atomList.length; j++) {
      const a1 = atomList[i], a2 = atomList[j];
      if (a1.atom === a2.atom && a1.power * a2.power < 0 && !a1.canceled && !a2.canceled) {
        a1.canceled = true; a2.canceled = true;
      }
    }
  }

  const allExprTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast)));
  for (const tok of allExprTokens) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") continue;

    if (uName === "deg" || uName === "°") {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = "°";
      continue;
    }

    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (uName === targetUnit) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (bag.substituteTex) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.substituteTex;
      continue;
    }

    const formatAtom = (atomName, p) => {
      const isCanceled = atomList.some(a => a.token === tok && a.atom === atomName && a.canceled);
      const expStr = Math.abs(p) > 1 ? `^{${Math.abs(p)}}` : "";
      const atomTex = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName]?.tex || `\\text{${atomName}}`;
      return isCanceled ? `\\cancel{${atomTex}}${expStr}` : `${atomTex}${expStr}`;
    };

    const numAtoms = [];
    const denAtoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      if (p > 0) numAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
      else if (p < 0) denAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
    }

    const numStr = numAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    const denStr = denAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    tokenMarkup[tok] = denStr ? `\\cfrac{${numStr || "1"}}{${denStr}}` : numStr;
  }

  return {
    tokenMarkup,
    conversionFactors: [],
    conversionFactorTex: "",
    numericConversionFactor,
    globalConversionTex,
    preConversions,
    tokenConversions
  };
}

/**
 * src/cas/units/analysis.js
 * Universal dimensional analysis:
 * - Cancel-first atomic cancellation across expression token bags
 * - Single global scale bracket collapse
 * - Universal projection for empirical relations: converts formula output unit into targetUnit
 * - Scaled dimensionless units (%, silentScale) fold into the numeric factor
 * - All TeX output passes through unitToTex
 */

import { parseMathAST, evalAST, getAstIdentifiers } from "../ast.js";
import { resolveCanonicalKey, isReservedMathToken } from "../algebra.js";
import { fmtSigFig } from "../../core/dimensions.js";
import { ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS, getUnitAtomBag, isDimEqual, unitToTex } from "./definitions.js";

export function evalExprUnitAST(node, S, varsDb) {
  if (!node) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "num") return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
  if (node.type === "id") {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(node.name, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[node.name] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[node.name]?.unit || "dimless";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    const atoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      const b = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[aName];
      atoms.push({
        token: node.name, canonicalKey: cKey, unitName: uName, atom: aName,
        dim: b?.dim || "custom", scale: b?.scale || 1.0, power: p, tex: b?.tex || `\\text{${aName}}`
      });
    }
    return { factor: bag.factor, atoms };
  }
  if (node.type === "group") return evalExprUnitAST(node.expr, S, varsDb);

  if (node.type === "call") {
    if (["sin", "cos", "tan"].includes(node.fn)) return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
    if (["atan", "acos", "asin"].includes(node.fn)) {
      return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [{ token: node.fn, canonicalKey: node.fn, unitName: "rad", atom: "rad", dim: "C", scale: 1.0, power: 1, tex: "\\text{rad}" }] };
    }
    if (node.fn === "sqrt") {
      const inner = evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
      return { factor: Math.sqrt(inner.factor), atoms: inner.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power / 2 })) };
    }
    return evalExprUnitAST(node.arg, S, varsDb);
  }

  if (node.type === "binary") {
    const left = evalExprUnitAST(node.left, S, varsDb);
    const right = evalExprUnitAST(node.right, S, varsDb);
    if (node.op === "*") return { factor: left.factor * right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms] };
    if (node.op === "/") return { factor: left.factor / right.factor, atoms: [...left.atoms, ...right.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: -a.power }))] };
    if (node.op === "+" || node.op === "-") return left;
    if (node.op === "^") {
      const pNum = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      return { factor: Math.pow(left.factor, pNum), atoms: left.atoms.map(a => ({ ...a, power: a.power * pNum })) };
    }
  }
  return { factor: 1.0, atoms: [] };
}

function collectTokenExponents(node, sign = 1, acc = {}) {
  if (!node) return acc;
  if (node.type === "group") return collectTokenExponents(node.expr, sign, acc);
  if (node.type === "id") {
    acc[node.name] = (acc[node.name] || 0) + sign;
    return acc;
  }
  if (node.type === "binary") {
    if (node.op === "*") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "/") {
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign, acc);
      collectTokenExponents(node.right, -sign, acc);
    } else if (node.op === "^") {
      const p = typeof evalAST(node.right, {}) === "number" ? evalAST(node.right, {}) : 1;
      collectTokenExponents(node.left, sign * p, acc);
    }
  }
  return acc;
}

export function solveUnitAlgebra(exprOrAST, S, targetVar, targetUnit, varsDb = {}, requiredUnits = null) {
  const ast = (typeof exprOrAST === "string") ? parseMathAST(exprOrAST) : exprOrAST;
  const tokenConversions = {};
  const preConversions = [];
  const tokenMarkup = {};

  // 1. Handling relations with explicit requiredUnits contracts
  if (requiredUnits && typeof requiredUnits === "object") {
    const varTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast).filter(t => !isReservedMathToken(t))));
    varTokens.forEach(tok => {
      const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
      const reqU = requiredUnits[cKey] || requiredUnits[tok];
      if (!reqU) return;
      const currU = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";

      if (currU === "°C" && (reqU === "K" || reqU === "degK")) {
        const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
        preConversions.push({
          token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
          conv: { factor: 1.0, latex: `(${fmtSigFig(rawVal)} + 273.15)\\,\\text{K}` },
          rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal + 273.15
        });
        return;
      }
      if (currU && reqU && currU !== reqU && currU !== "deg" && reqU !== "°") {
        const currBag = getUnitAtomBag(currU), reqBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqU);
        if (isDimEqual(currBag.dimMap, reqBag.dimMap)) {
          const ratio = currBag.scale / reqBag.scale;
          const rawVal = S[cKey] !== undefined ? S[cKey] : S[tok];
          preConversions.push({
            token: tok, canonicalKey: cKey, fromUnit: currU, toUnit: reqU,
            conv: { factor: ratio, latex: `\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqU)}}{\\cancel{${unitToTex(currU)}}}` },
            rawVal, convertedVal: rawVal * ratio
          });
        }
      }
    });

    // Universal target-side conversion for all empirical relations:
    // Compares formula's output unit (reqTargetU) with requested unit (targetUnit)
    const cTarget = resolveCanonicalKey(targetVar, varsDb);
    const reqTargetU = requiredUnits[cTarget] || requiredUnits[targetVar];
    let numericConversionFactor = 1.0;
    let globalConversionTex = "";

    if (reqTargetU && targetUnit && reqTargetU !== targetUnit) {
      const reqTargetBag = getUnitAtomBag(reqTargetU);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      if (isDimEqual(reqTargetBag.dimMap, targetBag.dimMap)) {
        numericConversionFactor = reqTargetBag.scale / targetBag.scale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          const ratio = numericConversionFactor;
          if (ratio < 1) {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{1\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{${fmtSigFig(1 / ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          } else {
            globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[\\cfrac{${fmtSigFig(ratio)}\\,${unitToTex(targetUnit)}}{1\\,${unitToTex(reqTargetU)}}\\right]`;
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (!reqTargetU && targetUnit) {
      const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
      const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);
      let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
      exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });
      const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
      if (targetScale > 0) {
        numericConversionFactor = exprScale / targetScale;
        if (Math.abs(numericConversionFactor - 1) > 1e-12) {
          globalConversionTex = `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(numericConversionFactor, 6)}\\right]`;
        }
      }
    }

    return { tokenMarkup, conversionFactors: [], conversionFactorTex: "", numericConversionFactor, globalConversionTex, preConversions, tokenConversions };
  }

  // 2. Pure physics dimensional analysis
  const exprAnalysis = evalExprUnitAST(ast, S, varsDb);
  const targetBag = getUnitAtomBag(targetUnit);

  let exprScale = exprAnalysis.factor;
  exprAnalysis.atoms.forEach(a => { exprScale *= Math.pow(a.scale, a.power); });

  const targetScale = targetBag.scale || 1.0;
  const numericConversionFactor = targetScale > 0 ? (exprScale / targetScale) : 1.0;

  let silentExpr = 1.0;
  const exps = collectTokenExponents(ast);
  for (const [tok, exp] of Object.entries(exps)) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) silentExpr *= Math.pow(bag.scale || 1, exp);
  }
  const silentTarget = targetBag.scaledDimensionless ? (targetBag.scale || 1) : 1;
  const silentPart = silentExpr / silentTarget;
  const bracketFactor = silentPart !== 0 ? numericConversionFactor / silentPart : numericConversionFactor;
  const globalConversionTex = (Math.abs(bracketFactor - 1) > 1e-12)
    ? `\\cdot\\left[${fmtSigFig(bracketFactor, 6)}\\right]`
    : "";

  const atomList = exprAnalysis.atoms.map((a, idx) => ({ ...a, id: idx, canceled: false }));
  for (let i = 0; i < atomList.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < atomList.length; j++) {
      const a1 = atomList[i], a2 = atomList[j];
      if (a1.atom === a2.atom && a1.power * a2.power < 0 && !a1.canceled && !a2.canceled) {
        a1.canceled = true; a2.canceled = true;
      }
    }
  }

  const allExprTokens = Array.from(new Set(getAstIdentifiers(ast)));
  for (const tok of allExprTokens) {
    const cKey = resolveCanonicalKey(tok, varsDb);
    const uName = S._u?.[cKey] || S._u?.[tok] || varsDb[cKey]?.unit || varsDb[tok]?.unit || "";
    if (!uName || uName === "dimless" || uName === "ratio") continue;

    if (uName === "deg" || uName === "°") {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = "°";
      continue;
    }

    const bag = getUnitAtomBag(uName);
    if (bag.scaledDimensionless) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (uName === targetUnit) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.displayTex || unitToTex(uName);
      continue;
    }
    if (bag.substituteTex) {
      tokenMarkup[tok] = bag.substituteTex;
      continue;
    }

    const formatAtom = (atomName, p) => {
      const isCanceled = atomList.some(a => a.token === tok && a.atom === atomName && a.canceled);
      const expStr = Math.abs(p) > 1 ? `^{${Math.abs(p)}}` : "";
      const atomTex = ATOMIC_BASE_UNITS[atomName]?.tex || `\\text{${atomName}}`;
      return isCanceled ? `\\cancel{${atomTex}}${expStr}` : `${atomTex}${expStr}`;
    };

    const numAtoms = [];
    const denAtoms = [];
    for (const [aName, p] of Object.entries(bag.atoms)) {
      if (p > 0) numAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
      else if (p < 0) denAtoms.push(formatAtom(aName, p));
    }

    const numStr = numAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    const denStr = denAtoms.join("\\cdot ");
    tokenMarkup[tok] = denStr ? `\\cfrac{${numStr || "1"}}{${denStr}}` : numStr;
  }

  return {
    tokenMarkup,
    conversionFactors: [],
    conversionFactorTex: "",
    numericConversionFactor,
    globalConversionTex,
    preConversions,
    tokenConversions
  };
}

│export function phi(n = 1) {
│  if (n === 0) return 1;
│  if (n === 1) return PHI_BASE;
│  if (n === -1) return 1 / PHI_BASE;
│  return Math.pow(PHI_BASE, n);
⋮
│export function fib(n) {
│  if (n <= 0) return 0;
│  if (n === 1) return 1;
│  let a = 0, b = 1;
│  for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
│    const c = a + b;
│    a = b;
│    b = c;
│  }
│  return b;

src/mastery/constants.js:

│export function phi(n = 1) {
│  if (n === 0) return 1;
│  if (n === 1) return PHI_BASE;
│  if (n === -1) return 1 / PHI_BASE;
│  return Math.pow(PHI_BASE, n);
⋮
│export function fib(n) {
│  if (n <= 0) return 0;
│  if (n === 1) return 1;
│  let a = 0, b = 1;
│  for (let i = 2; i <= n; i++) {
│    const c = a + b;
│    a = b;
│    b = c;
│  }
│  return b;
⋮
